Apparently Fetlife's own James Huesmann (who seems possessed of a deep understanding and given to militant respect for just some freedoms) believes that the previous volume of this publication "creates a likelihood of confusion, mistake and/or deception with the consuming public, which could be that the product is the same as FetLife, or that they are are somehow associated, affiliated, connected, approved,authorized or sponsored by FetLife".

Let us then make it perfectly clear :

  • That there is no association of any kind, nor will there ever be any sort of association. Due to the appalling and outright morally repugnant manner in which Bitlove conducts itself in the public space I would definitely not want my name in any substantial way related to Fetlife, nor can I imagine a circumstance in which upstanding, thinking individuals would ever wish to be associated with themi, outside of sheer ignorance ;

  • That this publication exists specifically and precisely because Fetlife - in a manner sorely reminiscent of a list of Bitcoin confidence men with business aspirations too lengthy to reproduce here - has been preying on a mostly unsuspecting public by making materially false claims and putting out a whole lot of puffery about security, while at the same time not merely refusing to work towards fixing problems, but outright trying to supress even discussion of problems, many of which are blatant ;

  • That arguments are not brought in the conditional-optative. If you wish to argue confusion, argue confusion, and show how it's supposed to work.ii Don't go "oh, it can be this, or that, or the other" in the hopes that nobody reads and you get your way in spite of truth and simple common decency. That's not a way to live, and no, having given at the office does not excuse you from behaving like something other than a collected sack of feces, Mr. "Ombudsman".

  2. People who argue with an explanation of the mechanism are generally sane adults who have specified, objective problems and are genuinely looking for solutions to them.

    On the other hand, people who argue with an armwave, "oh it could be this, or that, whatever" are generally infantile minds that object to the existence of the other in principle. Their wish is for whatever wounded their narcissism/contradicted their expectations/failed to match their preconceived notions to simply disappear, so they may be saved the intellectual effort of adjusting to reality.

    It is universally a bad idea to encourage the latter type. [↩]

So fucking convinced they are in those grease soaked noodles that play-pretend to be a brain that they should get away with being stupid and lazy, that the slightest upset of that expectation brings forth all the noisy aggitation of a five year old. The lazy, stupid five year old they still are.

So no, I don't wage war on "the army", the corporations, the governments, the whatever the fucks. I couldn't care less. The only corpses I wish to see belong to they whom you call "civillians" and I affectionately refer to as "filth". Once that's washed out into the sewer, both corporations and governments - along with everything else - will do just fine. For as long as they're still here, befouling the air, besmirching the land, with their "needs" and "expectations" and whatnot, you can't really get any better than Clinton and Trump engaged in a pie throwing contest.

I find the delusion that you get to ignore gunpoints endearing. Nice super power, that, howdja get it ? Keep up with it, from what I hear it's what most corpses have in common.
